# Results & Calibration Ledger
<!-- ledger-epoch: 2026 -->  <!-- season anchor for pulse.py's M/D year inference — do not remove -->

**Purpose.** The quantitative twin of `fades.md`. Every **recommended leg** (and especially every
**played** leg) gets logged here with my **true-prob estimate**, the **price**, the **closing
price** (CLV), and the **result** — so we can measure, not guess, how good the process is:
calibration (do my "70%" legs hit ~70%?), hit rate & ROI by bet type, and closing-line value.

**Columns.**
- **TrueP** = my **pre-bet** true-win-prob estimate — **written at bet time, never reconstructed.** A
  back-filled number leaks the result and is calibration-invalid. Rows I genuinely didn't log pre-game are
  marked `*` and EXCLUDED from calibration. *Goal: zero new `*` rows* (11 of 21 historical legs were wasted
  this way → calibration ran on n=10).
- **ImplP** = the price's **NO-VIG** implied prob. **Devig before logging:** take both sides' raw implied
  probs and divide each by their sum (the overround); that's the no-vig number. One-sided prop with no
  posted other side → subtract ~half the typical hold (~2-3pp) and flag the estimate. *(Pre-6/4 rows are
  still raw-vig and labeled; recompute as they settle.)*
- **Edge** = TrueP − no-vig ImplP (pp). Positive = value. A leg must clear the **min-edge gate** to be bet:
  **≥ +2pp standalone / ≥ +3-4pp to anchor a parlay** — below that it's action, not value (a slate clearing
  nothing = NO BET).
- **Result** = W / L / Push / TBD. **Played** = Y if on the user's actual ticket, N if recommended/rejected only.
- **Bucket** = **S** (standalone single / bankroll roll) or **P** (rode on a parlay ticket). Lets `calib.py`
  break out calibration & record by bucket — the measurement that *proves or disproves the parlay tax*
  (doctrine claims parlays run near -EV chalk+vig; this is how we test it). Going forward the diversify-markets
  direction should grow the **S** sample (more Tier-1 standalones), so the split becomes real.
- **Close** = closing American price; **CLV** = did the line move toward our side (closing no-vig ImplP >
  bet no-vig ImplP)? `+` good / `−` bad / `—` not captured. **CLV converges far faster than ROI at this
  sample — it's the primary scoreboard.** *(Capture starts now; pre-6/4 rows are `—`.)*
- **Stake / Return / P-L** = units staked, units returned (stake × decimal on a win, 0 on a loss), and
  net P/L (units). Tracked per *ticket* in the rollup → running units & ROI. **Log the real stake each
  night;** pre-6/4 rows assume flat 1u (labeled) so there's an immediate read.

---

## Logging protocol (run EVERY session)
1. **On build:** add a row for each recommended leg (Played=N) with price, **pre-registered TrueP**,
   **no-vig ImplP**, Edge. **Pull the price from a real book — never estimate. Devig both sides before
   computing Edge. Write TrueP BEFORE the game — never reconstruct** (a `*` row is wasted calibration).
2. **At/near close (HARD step — do it on the run closest to first pitch):** record the **closing price**
   and compute CLV (`+` if the line moved to our side, comparing no-vig probs). **CLV is the primary
   scoreboard — it converges far faster than ROI at this sample** — capture it even on legs we don't bet.
   *(`tools/odds_api.sh clv <price> "<team>"` automates this per ML leg — or run `tools/clv_capture.py` on the 15:30/18:30 run to batch-propose all open-leg CLV fills in one shot. Props / totals / K-legs remain a manual book pull.)*
3. **On settle:** set Result (W/L/Push), flip Played=Y for legs the user actually played, and for each
   **played ticket** record **stake + return** → update the units/ROI rollup. Self-settle via
   `mlb_api.sh finals` on the 09:00 review like the parlay files. **Verify the rollups with `tools/calib.py`.**
4. **Commit** this file in the same commit→push→PR→squash-merge cycle as the parlay/fade files.

Because `calib.py`, `pulse.py` and `clv_capture.py` all parse **by section heading**, **17 leg rows were invisible to every measurement tool** — including yesterday's four settled K-Unders — and today's nine open legs could not be CLV-captured at all (`clv_capture.py` reported 'no open TBD legs'). The rows were moved into the correct section; effect: pulse window **46→48** decided legs (surfacing a new `type:K-Under 3-1` dimension), calib Brier sample **122→124**, `adj:custom` **14→16 (10-6)**, and **9 CLV verdicts recovered** that would otherwise have been lost. **All four pulse ACTIONS were unchanged by the fix**, so no Build A decision is retroactively invalidated. `selftest.sh` did not catch this because its dashboard parser guard asserts row *counts*, not row *placement* — a placement assertion is the obvious follow-up hardening. **8/3 settled + RECONCILED to `calib.py` 2026-08-04 11:00 (Build A):** 25 open 8/3 rows closed — Schlittler O6.5K ❌ L (**6 K, one short**, and it killed the Tier-1 standalone AND all three ticket versions), King O4.5K ✅ W (5 K), TB@COL Over 11.5 ✅ W (**22 runs**), Nola U5.5K ✅ W (5 K, correctly declined as −EV), WSH@PHI Over 9.0 ➖ Push (exactly 9), TOR@HOU Over 9.0 ❌ L (**4 runs** — the pulse band-shade that barred it was right), PIT@MIL Over 8.5 ❌ L (7), SF@TEX Over 8.0 ❌ L (6), McGreevy U4.5K ❌ L (5 K), Wrobleski O5.5K ❌ L (4 K), Seymour U5.5-hits ❌ L (7 H), and both Angle-B Overs ✅ would-W (Nola 6 H, Lorenzen 7 H). **The 15-favourite row scored 3-5** — chalk lost the majority on an 8-game board while every one of them sat pulse-shaded to 0.0pp. **⚠ SECOND LEDGER STRUCTURAL FIX this week, same failure class:** `calib.py`'s `parse_result` needs the W/L/Push token to appear **first** inside the bold verdict wrapper, so **80 verdict markers written as `**✅ WON**` / `**❌ LOST**` parsed to `None` and were invisible to every calibration and governor read.** All normalized to `**W** ✅ WON` / `**L** ❌ LOST`. Effect: Brier-scored legs **124 → 148** (skill **+0.0037 → +0.0043**), §4 recommended-not-played **63-36 → 90-62 (59% would-win)**, `adj:custom` **16 → 19 (11-8, 58%)**, and pulse's decided-leg window **48 → 56**. **Unlike the 8/3 fix, this one DID change the build:** the wider window fired a brand-new **`COOL type:K-Over ≤6.5` (2-3, 40% vs 56% claimed)**, which zeroed a +8.0pp Brady Singer leg and replaced a **+231 / 38.3%-floor** ticket with **+256 / 33.5%**. Two consecutive runs have now found a silent parse/placement bug that materially moved the numbers the governor acts on; `selftest.sh` checks row *counts* and section *placement* but nothing asserts that **every non-TBD verdict cell actually parses to W/L/Push** — that assertion is the next hardening and would have caught both. **8/6 RECONCILED to `calib.py` 2026-08-06 16:25 (Build B) — and this run found the THIRD silent parse bug in four days, the largest of the three.** `calib.py`'s `parse_pct` tested `starred = "*" in s` to detect the ledger's legacy reconstructed-TrueP marker (`~72%*`), but a bare `"*"` **also matches the markdown bold markers in a cell like `**54.5%**`** — so **every bold-wrapped TrueP was misread as a calibration-invalid legacy row and silently discarded** from the bands, from §1b, and (same ledger) from `pulse.py`'s window. Because the build writes its **headline Tier-1 and ticket legs in bold**, the loss was biased toward exactly the rows that matter most. **Effect of the fix: Brier-scored legs 172 → 221, and the skill flipped from +0.0036 ("TrueP BEATS the logged price") to −0.0004 ("TrueP does NOT beat the logged price"); pulse's decided-leg window 77 → 120; pulse actions 10 → 12.** ⚠ **The single most-quoted claim in this repo — that the written adjustments add signal over the no-vig market — was an artifact of hiding 49 rows.** On the corrected full sample our TrueP is indistinguishable from (a hair worse than) the market price. **Like the 8/3 fix, this one DID change the build, and terminally:** the newly-visible rows fired **`MARKET-SHADE type:total` (CLV 5+/7−)** and **`MARKET-SHADE band:50-54` (CLV 10+/15−)**, which double-shaded the day's only surviving leg (SD@AZ Over 9.0) from +3.0pp to **0.0pp** — making 8/6 a **NO BET slate across all three tiers.** Newly visible and directly relevant: **`adj:hitter_park_over` 4-4 (50% vs 52% claimed, skill −0.0005)** and **`adj:pitcher_park_under` 2-3 (40% vs 52%, skill −0.0066)** — both park tags, the entire source of every "+3.0pp" in Build A, measure at or below zero. Settled 8/6 rows: LAA@BAL Over 9.5 **L** (5 runs — the pulse band-kill was CORRECT, and the refused baseline-shop would have lost at +4.0pp), ATH@CIN Over 9.5 **W** (11 runs — the deliberate Tier-1 pass missed), NYM@CLE Under 7.5 **L** (**19 runs**, killing the Tier 2/3 ticket), Cease O7.5K **W** and O6.5K alt **W** (**10 K** — the governor was wrong twice on the same arm; `fades.md` C6 flagged). Bands unchanged at n=31 played / 20 won (all new rows Played=N); §4 recommended-not-played **115-102 → 118-105**. **HARDENING SHIPPED THIS RUN (the assertion the previous two fixes each called for):** `selftest.sh` now asserts that a bold-wrapped TrueP parses as **not** starred and that a `**W** ✅ WON`-style verdict parses to W — field-level parse assertions, not just row counts/placement. `selftest.sh` **88/88**. **8/6 FULLY SETTLED + RECONCILED to `calib.py` 2026-08-07 11:00 (Build A).** All 14 open 8/6 rows closed off the finals. DET@SEA Under 8.0 **L** (11 runs — Valdez's flagged one-sided blow-up risk is exactly what happened); the Tier 2/3 ticket **L** twice over; CWS@BOS Over 9.0 **W** (**23 runs**) and WSH@PHI Over 9.0 **W** (10 runs) — ⚠ **both of these were legs the 16:00 run discretionarily killed on narrative grounds (a fresh weather read, a line move away from us), so mechanical governance went 1-0 while in-run human kills went 0-2 on a single slate**; MIN@KC Under 9.0/8.5 **W** (7 runs, band-blocked); the declined-adjustment control group **1-2** (declining cost nothing); **the 11-favourite row scored 5-6** — a sub-.500 chalk day that is the **second consecutive vindication of the `type:ML-fav` MARKET-SHADE** after 8/3's 3-5, breaking the 8/4–8/5 run of 10-5 days; both Angle B candidates **would-W** (Mikolas **12 H** vs O6.5, Castillo **6 H** vs O5.5), taking **Angle B to 5-0 would-have** — every candidate ever shaded to no-bet on hook-risk has cashed, which makes the shade magnitude, not the thesis, the thing to revisit. **Reconciled numbers:** bands unchanged at **n=31 played / 20 won** (all new rows Played=N; 55-59 at 43% ⚠, 60-64 64%, 65-69 86%); Brier-scored legs **221 → 228** with skill **−0.0004 → −0.0005** (TrueP still does not beat the logged price); §1c `hitter_park_over` **8 → 12 (6-6, skill −0.0004)** and `pitcher_park_under` **5 → 6 (2-4, skill −0.0107)**; §4 recommended-not-played **118-105 → 125-109 (53% would-win)**; pulse window **120 → 127** decided legs, actions **12 → 11**. **Two governor changes fell out of the settle:** `type:total` came **OFF** the MARKET-SHADE (CLV 5+/7− → **9+/7−**, 21-17) and `adj:pitcher_park_under` went **ON** to COOL (2-4, 33% vs 53%). **Doctrine promoted this run (3rd sighting, process-lesson bar):** park tags (`hitter_park_over` / `pitcher_park_under`) are **TIEBREAKERS ONLY** — they may break a tie between two legs that each already carry a real matchup thesis, and may never manufacture an edge or anchor a Tier 1. Recorded in `fades.md`.
**User's own 6/6 tickets (logged separately, not from a build): Ticket 1 ✅ +$20.62; Ticket 2 2/3 locked, LAD −1.5 live (eff. W).**

> **6/5 is the parlay tax, on the board:** the LAD leg cashed standalone (bankroll won) but the SEA×LAD
> *parlay* lost. Two individually-+EV favorites stacked = ~38% combined → the ticket dies the majority of
> nights regardless of leg quality. Standalone-vs-parlay EV split now reads: **standalone bankroll/Tier-1
> legs trending +, parlay tickets 5-7 / ROI bleeding toward breakeven** — the doctrine's "parlays are
> near-EV chalk+vig" claim is accumulating evidence.

> **6/4 standalone (separate from the parlay table):** Sale Over 6.5 K −179 — **LOST** (5.2 IP / 6 K).
> First K-Over loss in the log (played K-Overs now **5-for-6**). The lone suppressor (TOR 19% K / .213
> vs LHP) was real, and the **one-tier-deeper alt O5.5 would have cashed** — a live confirmation of the
> safety-vs-EV tiebreaker (the deeper alt on an unattended K leg).

> ⚠️ **Read this ROI as noise, not signal.** n=10 is variance-dominated and the +56.8% is carried by two
> ~+260 hits (5/31, 5/25); a single different bounce flips it. Stakes here are **assumed flat 1u** (not
> actually logged) — log the real stake each night so this becomes a true number. **This is exactly why
> CLV (above) matters more than ROI at this sample** — beat the close consistently and the ROI follows.

### Now capturing / still to capture
- **Stake + units/ROI** — now in the ticket rollup (flat-1u assumed pre-6/4; **log the real stake going
  forward** so ROI stops being an assumption).

  Still a manual book pull (no odds API).

  would've cashed), while the **60-64 ML-fav band is 50%** (overbet). The 3-tier output exists to surface
  the K-Over standalone as the real product, not just the +200 parlay.

## User-angle tracking (started 6/7/26 — user request)
Two angles the user gravitates to that aren't surfaced by the standard build. **Tracking dimension only**
— these rows ALSO live in the main "Played legs" table (tagged `*` until pre-registered); this section is
the per-angle scoreboard. Each run: when one of these appears on the board, note it here even if not bet,
and log W/L on settle. **Both are noise until N≥20-30 — directional only, do NOT size off them yet.**

### Angle A — LIVE moneyline (in-game ML, taken after first pitch)
The 6/6 TOR leg was a live-ML (−191, taken once TOR led). Thesis to test: does taking a fav's ML LIVE
(better price after an early lead, or a value re-entry on an early deficit) beat the pregame number?
Capture: pregame ML vs the live price taken (the "live CLV"), the game state at entry, result.
| Date | Game | Live price | Entry state | Pregame ML (ref) | Result | Live-CLV |
|------|------|-----------|-------------|------------------|--------|----------|
| 6/6 | TOR ML (vs BAL) | -191 | led early | ~-132 (open) | **W** (TOR 6-4) | took worse # for safety — live tax, but cashed |
| 6/7 | LAD (vs LAA, Sheehan/Soriano) | WATCH | re-entry IF trails early | pull at game time | **no-trigger** (LAD lost 5-13 — a deficit re-entry would have LOST; correct to pass) | candidate #1 — strong team, moderate fav; trail early → overshoot |
| 6/7 | NYY (vs BOS, Suarez LHP) | WATCH | re-entry IF trails early | -160 (open) | **no-trigger** (NYY won 6-1 wire-to-wire — no early deficit, no re-entry spot) | candidate #2 — Suarez tough; early NYY deficit = live value |
| 6/7 | ATL (vs PIT, Bryce Elder) | WATCH | re-entry IF trails early | -145 (open) | **no-trigger** (ATL won 3-2 close — pregame ML cashed; settled in main table) | candidate #3 — **probable is Elder (2.63), not Chandler** (pre-seed corrected); ATL also Build A Tier 2/3 anchor |
| 8/3 | (none armed) | — | — | — | **no candidate** — the pulse `type:ML-fav` MARKET-SHADE zeroes every favorite's pregame edge, so there is no fav worth re-entering *from*. Reference board = the 16:00 `best h2h` pull in `parlays/2026-08-03.md` | — |
| 8/5 | (none armed) | — | — | ref board 16:15: MIL -124, ATL -122, SD -122, BOS -126, CIN -147, SEA -150, NYY -153 | **no candidate** — third slate running with the same cause: `type:ML-fav` MARKET-SHADED, so no favourite carries a pregame edge to re-enter *from*. Refs captured anyway so an in-game entry has a baseline | **n/a — none armed** |

**Angle A record: 1-0** (6/7 = 3 pre-game WATCH candidates, value-re-entry type — execute in-game only). Watch: live-ML usually means PAYING UP for a lead already in (the −132→−191
move is the "live tax"). The edge case worth proving is the OPPOSITE — a value re-entry when a good team
falls behind early and the live price overshoots. Flag which type each entry is.

### Angle B — opposing-SP hits-allowed OVER (fade-the-bad-matchup hits prop)
The 6/6 Kochanowicz O5.5 hits cleared when he was shelled (0.1 IP / 6 ER). Thesis: a hits-allowed Over on
a vulnerable SP vs a strong contact offense is a softer market than the K-props/ML. Capture: SP, line,
opposing lineup contact quality, result (hits allowed).

| 8/3 | **Nola O5.5 hits allowed (PHI, vs WSH)** | **+110** (Bovada/DK/Fanatics/BetMGM); best Under **-146** DK → devig **44.5% / 55.5%** | λ≈**5.8 hits** (1.45 WHIP × **5.3 IP/start** − ~1.9 BB) → Poisson P(≥6)=**52.2%**, 7.7pp over market; WSH offense mid (55-58, RDiff +16) | **NO BET — 0.0pp** after `market_disagrees-4` + `custom-2` hook shade | **would-W** ✅ — 6 hits allowed — **⚠ HOOK RISK HIGH was the right worry but did not bite**: a 5.61-ERA arm is exactly the one pulled at 4 IP when hit, which CAPS hits allowed. Three straight candidates we shaded to no-bet on hook risk have cashed; at N=3 that is directional, but the shade magnitude is now the thing to watch, not the thesis.**

| 8/5 | Irvin O4.5 hits allowed (WSH, @ PHI) | -148; Under +120 → devig **56.8% / 43.2%** (hold 5.1%) | Correlates POSITIVELY with our own Tier-1 Over 9.5 — the interesting part — but Irvin is 2 starts into a post-IL return, i.e. the same short-leash left tail that has eaten every prior candidate | **NO BET — priced + left tail** | **L** (3 H allowed) |

**Angle B record: 1-0** (6/7 = 3 WATCH candidates; pull the real line + devig near first pitch — Gilbert/PHI passed: opener/start-length unknown). Watch: (a) hits-allowed correlates with IP — a quick hook (few outs) can CAP
hits even in a blowup, so it has the same left-tail start-length risk as K-Overs; (b) books shade these
up on public "blowup" narratives. Pull the real number, devig, and treat like a K-Over alt decision.
